股價相對大盤走勢

台積電 (2330 TT)  Buy

競爭力佳，建議 Buy，目標價 3,200 元。  
投資建議 ：台積電將有望再提高 2024~2029 年  AI 加速器和
CPU 等營收之年複合成長率  (CAGR) 55% - 59% 。即使消費性
電子（如  PC 、智慧型手機）等價格敏感市場受到零件漲價與總
經不確定性影響，但 AI 需求極度強勁。由於 AI 正從生成式 （詢
問模式）轉向代理式 /專注型（指令與行動模式） ， 大幅增加Token
的消耗量與運算需求，並增加 CPU 的需求。台積電將 2026 年
美元營收成長率預期上修， YoY>+40% 。預估台積電 2026 年稅
後 EPS 106.46 元。隨 CSP 大廠進行大規模布局，有利台積電訂
單增加，且競爭力佳，投資評等 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 27.25 元：2Q26 營收為 1.27 兆元 ，QoQ+12.02% ，
美元營收達 402.01 億美元， QoQ+11.99% ，皆優於財測上緣。
產能利用率高，且公司持續改善成本，使台積電毛利率較 1Q26
提升 1.47 個百分點至 67.72% ，營業利益率提升至 60.34% 。台
積電 2Q26 稅後 EPS 27.25 元。  
上修 2026 年資本支出至 600~640 億美元 ： AI 需求持續，台積
電提高 2026 年本支出 600–640 億美元 ，YoY+46.72%~+56.50% ，
較原先預估增加約 15%。其中先進製程 N3 和 N2 製程產能的增
加將佔總資本支出比重 70~80% 。隨著 N3 產能持續放量，以及
N2 製程於 2025 年底開始量產，並配合美國與日本海外晶圓廠
逐步進入量產階段。  
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 27.51 元：高階製程持續帶動台積電營收成
長，台積電預估 3Q26 毛利率將介於  65% - 67% ，以中間值  
66% 計算，較  2Q26 下降約  1.7 個百分點。主要是因為 N2 技
術的快速爬坡，預期將稀釋毛利率約  3 - 4 個百分點，此稀釋
將被領先技術的強勁需求與持續的成本改善（含生產力提升）
部分抵消。預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 27.51 元。

2 群益投顧 
  台積電 ESG ：  
ESG 政策重點  
•  2025 年起，所有關鍵排放源供應商必須簽署溫室氣體減排協議，承諾
2030 年達成減排目標，且 2026 年底前要完成產品碳足跡第三方驗證，
不合規者將減少合作機會。  
•  將供應鏈減碳績效全面納入選商評估，每年須提出新年度與中長期減排
計畫，定期檢討減碳進度，強化全產業鏈去碳化管理。  
•  推出供應鏈減碳行動指引，包括績效追蹤與提高透明度、資源支援與輔
導、提升動機與擴大影響範圍、合作創新與成本效益管理四大面向。  
近期 ESG 行動與報告  
•  台積電發表《永續報告書》，強化 ESG 治理架構及與利害關係人合作，
揭露永續進展。  
•  擴大永續報告內容，聚焦九項聯合國 SDGs 與多元共融、多語、無障礙
專案，包括女性領導力、多元文化主題活動和身心障礙者職場體驗，提
高員工共融意識。  
•  ESG 官網於 2024 年底改版，減少數位能耗 9%、碳排放 28%，網站對身
心障礙者資訊近用性提升，導入多語系發展及多元功能服務。  
長期承諾與具體成果  
•  加速 RE100 進程 ， 將全球營運100% 再生能源目標從 2050 年提前至 2040
年， 2030 年生產據點再生能源比例提升至 60% ，並推出減碳補助專案
總額 8,400 萬元，帶動供應商節電、節水、減廢。  
•  治理政策由 ESG 指導委員會及 ESG 委員會主導，確立重大議題盤點與
風險機會管理，每年發布永續報告書作為管理工具，連結高階主管薪酬
與 ESG 成果，並透過 ESG AWARD 獎勵機制推動內部文化  
•  秉持創辦人張忠謀價值觀（誠信正直、強化環保、關懷弱勢），跨部門
督導 23 項永續作為及 57 個長期目標，對應九個聯合國 SDGs ，確保透
明揭露與利害關係人溝通。  
•  強化全球價值鏈治理，呼應國際 ESG 倡議，持續精進評比成績，如連
續入選道瓊永續指數。  
N2 開始貢獻營收，台積電 2Q26 營收 1.27 兆元， QoQ+12.02% ，稅後淨利
7,065.62 億元， QoQ+23.42% ，稅後 EPS 27.25 元：  
台積電產能利用率維持高檔，受惠於 N3、 N5 等 AI 與高效能運算（ HPC ）
需求支撐， 2Q26 營收為 1.27 兆元， QoQ+12.02% ，稅後淨利 7,065.62 億元，
QoQ+23.42% ，稅後 EPS 27.25 元。  
2Q26 美元營收達 402.01 億美元， QoQ+11.99% ，優於財測上緣，台積電原
預估營收 390~402 億美元， QoQ+10.69%~+12.37% ，中間值 +9.01% 。2Q26 實

3 群益投顧 
際匯率為新台幣 31.60 元兌 1 美元，較 1Q26 的 31.59 元貶值 0.03% 。 2Q26
出貨量為 433.6 萬片約當 12 吋晶圓，較 1Q26 的 417.4 萬片， QoQ+3.88% 。
製程結構方面， 2Q26 N2 首度貢獻營收，比重達 3%；N3 營收比重由  1Q26 
的  25% 提升至  30% ；N5 營收比重由  36% 降至  33% ；N7 為  11% ；
N16/N20 為  6% ；N28 為  6% 。N7 及更先進製程合計占晶圓營收比重達
77% 。  
依產品應用別， 2Q26 以數位消費性電子成長最大， QoQ+28% ，占營收比重
1%；HPC QoQ+20% ，營收比重 61%；智慧型手機 QoQ-11% ，營收比重 26%；
車用電子 QoQ-7% ，營收比重 4%；物聯網（ IoT ）相關產品 QoQ+12% ，營收
比重 6%；其他應用 QoQ+17% ，營收比重 1%。  
依產品應用別， 2Q26 以  HPC （高效能運算）成長最大， QoQ +20% ，占營
收比重達  66% ；車 用 電 子 QoQ +15% ， 營收比重 4% ； 其他應用 QoQ +5% ，
營收比重  2% ；數位消費性電子  (DCE) QoQ +5% ，營收比重  1% ；物聯
網  (IoT) QoQ+4% ，營收比重  5% ；而智慧型手機則因季節性因素  QoQ -
4% ，營收比重降至  22% 。  
毛利率方面， 2Q26 受惠於成本改善努力與較高的產能利用率，實際毛利率
較  1Q26 提升  1.47 個百分點至  67.72% 。營業費用  QoQ 增加  49.7 億
元至  989.8 億元  ，但得益於營業槓桿效益，營業利益率提升至  60.34% 。
實際毛利率與營業利益率皆高於原先財測區間（毛利率  65.5% ~ 67.5% ，
營益率  56.5% ~ 58.5% ）。 業外收益方面 ， 整體業外淨收益達 958.3 億元  ，
其中利息淨收入約為  269.6 億元  ，另有處分及評價世界先進  (VIS) 股份
之收益  632 億元。

資料來源：台積電，群益預估彙整 
台積電提高 2026 年資本支出至 600-640 億美元， YoY+46.72%~+56.50% ，
較原先預估 520-560 億美元，增加約 15% ：  
台積電 2025 年資本支出為 408.95 億美元，較 2024 年的 297.60 億美元，
YoY+37.42% 。 AI 需求持續強勁、先進製程與海外擴產同步推進的背景，另
還有通膨壓力，台積電提高 2026 年資本支出至 600–640 億美元，
YoY+46.72%~+56.50% ，較原先預估 520-560 億美元，增加約 15% 。反映公
司對中長期先進製程與全球製造布局的持續投入。就資本配置結構而言，
2026 年資本支出中約 70-80% 將用於先進製程 （N3 與 N2） 產能擴充，約10%

4 群益投顧 
投入特殊製程，其餘約 10–20% 配置於後段先進封裝、測試、光罩製作及其
他項目。公司指出，資本支出規劃係透過嚴謹的產能規劃系統，與超過 500
家客戶密切合作，結合 Top-down 與 Bottom-up 方式評估需求，並依據技術
領先、具彈性且可回應市場需求的製造能力、維持客戶信任，以及取得適當
報酬等原則，決定最適當的投資規模。  
台積電全球目前正在興建或改建的廠房達 18 座（包含  5 座先進封裝廠） ，
其中 12~13 座位於台灣。擴廠速度已倍增至每年  9 座新廠（ 2025–2026 
年），對比過去的每年  4 座（ 2017–2024 年），己呈倍數成長。  
從資本密集度角度觀察，台積電近年資本密集度隨產業循環與製程轉換呈
現波動。過去五年中， 2021 年資本密集度曾高於 50% ，其後分別約 47% 與
43% ， 2024 年和 2025 年降至約 33% 。公司預估 2026 年資本密集度將回升
至 35 – 37% ，主要反映先進製程（ N3/N2 ）持續擴產及海外晶圓廠投資比重
提高。隨著美國、日本與歐洲廠區陸續進入建設與量產階段，未來數年資本
密集度仍可能維持相對高檔。  
台積電過去 5 年資本支出總計  1,670 億美元；研發投入總計  300.00 億美
元。 2023~2025 年資本支出約  1,010 億美元，未來 3 年預計將顯著提高。維
持長期毛利率目標  56.00% 或更高， ROE 目標維持在  20.00% 以上。並維
持穩定且可持續增加的現金股利。台積電 2024 年和 2025 年分別支付 14 元
和 18 元，預估 2026 年將支付現金股利 24 元， 2027 年將會持續增加。  
折舊費用方面，台積電 2025 年折舊費用為 6,880.93 億元，較 2024 年的
6,627.96
億元， YoY+3.82% ，低於原先市場預期約 10% 的年增幅，主因公司
前期審慎控管資本支出節奏。 2025 年折舊主要來自 N3 製程量產。隨著 N3
產能持續放量，以及 N2 製程於 2025 年底開始量產，並配合美國與日本海
外晶圓廠逐步進入量產階段 ， 台積電預估 2026 年折舊費用將 YoY+17~19% ，
折舊壓力將持續存在。  
折舊與資本密集度提升確實為公司毛利率的重要影響因子，但毛利率表現
同時取決於產品組合、定價、匯率、產能利用率及先進製程良率爬升速度等
多重因素。隨著 N3 製程進入成熟量產階段，其單位成本與折舊對毛利率的
稀釋效應預期將逐步下降； N2 製程雖於初期對毛利率造成壓力，但其定價
結構優於 N3，有助於中長期毛利率結構的穩定。台積電將持續透過成本改
善、產能利用率提升及價值定價策略，以抵銷高資本密集度與海外擴產帶來
的不利影響。  
打破過去單一節點達標後即不擴產的慣例，提升 N3 產能：  
就先進製程擴產進度而言，台積電目前先進製程投資重點聚焦於 N3 產能擴
充及 N2 產能建置。 N3 製程已於南科 Fab 18 進入量產並持續擴充，為 2024–
2025 年營收與折舊的主要來源之一。 N2 製程方面， 2025 年於新竹寶山 Fab 
20 啟動投片，並於 2H25 開放客戶預訂；高雄楠梓 Fab 22 於 2H25 試產並逐
步放量，未來將成為 N2 及後續先進製程的重要量產基地。 2025 年底 N2 月
產能 15~20K 片，預估 2026 年將達 120K/ 月。寶山 P2 廠 2026 年開始裝機。
至於高雄 P3 廠 2026 年完工並申領執照，規劃同年量產。 P4、P5 廠預計 2027
年辦理竣工及申領使照，若對應製程藍圖，屆時將可量產 N2P、N2X 甚至更
先進的 A16 埃米時代製程。

### Page 5

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 7 月 17 日

5 群益投顧 
為滿足 AI 在智慧型手機、 HPC 及 HBM 相關應用的強勁需求，台積電打破
過去單一節點達標後即不擴產的慣例，全面提升 N3 產能。包含：在台南科
學園區新增一座 Fab 投入 N3 生產，預計 1H27 量產。美國亞利桑那州第二
座晶圓廠完工，將導入 N3 技術， 2H27 量產。日本第二座晶圓廠也計畫採
用 N3 技術，預計 2028 年量產。同時也在內部進行設備轉換，將部分 N5 機
台轉為 N3，並在 N7、N5、N3 之間進行彈性產能支援。 2025 年底台積電 N3
月產能為 120K 片， 2026 年可再拉升至 150K 片 /月或更多。  
成熟製程產能增加， CoWoS 等後段封裝亦持續擴產：  
成熟製程策略維持不變，專注於高附加價值領域，例如透過日本 JASM 一廠
擴充 CMOS 影像感測器產能，以及德國 ESMC 的車用和工業應用，而非擴
充需求較弱的大宗商品成熟製程。僅 AI 相關 (電源管理 IC、感測器 )處於短
缺。其他消費性相關需求不強，整體並非大幅短缺。同時，台積電計畫逐步
關閉 Fab 2 （ 6 吋廠）與 Fab 5 （ 8 吋廠），將廠區空間騰出以支援先進製程
的發展。  
CoWoS 產能在 2026 年由原先預期的月產能 110~120K 片 ， 增加至130~140K
片，新增產能主要來自南科群創舊廠 AP8 廠，以及台中 AP5 。嘉義 AP7 廠
為最新且規模最大的先進封裝據點，整體規劃 8 座廠， P1 為 Apple 專屬
WMCM 產線； P2、 P3 以 SoIC 為主；為實現更大尺寸之  CoWoS 封裝，技
術藍圖已規劃至超過  14 倍光罩尺寸，台積電正開發替代方案以降低成本，
並與載板供應商合作。公司目前正在建立一條試產線，該產線約需要一年時
間才能成熟，面板級封裝 CoPoS 則暫定 P4 或 P5，預計 2028 年量產。鄰近
亞利桑那州廠的 2 座先進封裝廠也已整地中，建廠時程提前至 2H26 ，進機
時程約 1H28，2028 年底以 SoIC、CoW 的 AP1 廠應能開始少量生產， CoPoS
估計最快 2029 年加入。  
CoWoS 技術是 AI 訓練及推論的關鍵驅動。 2026 年台積電宣布生產全球最
大的 5.5 倍光罩尺寸 CoWoS ，其良率超過 98% 。在未來五年內， CoWoS 技
術將持續以每年放大尺寸的節奏發展，以整合更多的邏輯和 HBM 晶粒。整
合 20 個 HBM 的 14 倍光罩尺寸 CoWoS 將於 2028 年量產；而整合 24 個
HBM 、大於 14 倍光罩尺寸的版本則預計於 2029 年就緒。  
系統級晶圓 SoW(system-on-wafer) 中介層尺寸將超過 40 倍光罩尺寸，可整
合 64 個 HBM 與 16 個運算晶片，用於邏輯晶粒整合的 SoW-P 已自 2024 年
起開始量產，整合邏輯與 HBM 的 SoW-X 技術預計於 2029 年就緒。  
SoIC (system-on-integrated-chips) 方面 ， 相較於2.5D 互連的 CoWoS，3D SoIC
提供 56 倍的連接密度與 5 倍的功耗效率。 N7 on N7 SoIC(9 μm bond pitch)
已自 2023 年開始量產， 6μm 則已於 2025 年進入量產。 N2 on N2(6 μm bond 
pitch) 預計 2028 年開始量產， 2029 年 A14 on A14 堆疊 (4.5 μm bond pitch) 。
在共封裝光學（ CPO ）與矽光子技術方面，目前已展開小規模生產並將逐步
爬坡，預期未來幾年內將對營收產生實質貢獻。  
台積電宣布在美國再加碼 1,000 億美元，投資美國總金額將達 2,650 億美
元，目前海外廠皆照原先進度進行中：  
台積電持續推動全球布局，以因應供應鏈區域化趨勢並降低營運風險，目前

6 群益投顧 
海外廠皆依原定進度推進。  
美國 方面，台積電原先計劃於亞利桑那州總投資金額將達 1,650 億美元，涵
蓋 6 座晶圓廠、 2 座先進封裝設施及 1 座主要研發中心。現在將在美國亞利
桑那州再加碼投資 1,000 億美元，估計將再建置 4 座晶圓廠，以及先進封裝
廠，這將有助於促進美國半導體生態系統發展，強化供應鏈，滿足美國客戶
未來多年的強勁需求。 Fab 21 第一期已在 4Q24 以 N4 製程量產，月產能
20K；第二期建築已完工，預計  2026 年  開始機台移入與安裝， 2H27 進入
大規模量產。預計量產 N2；第三期 2025 年底已動工， 2030 年導入更先進
製程。第四座晶圓廠及首座先進封裝廠：正在申請相關許可證明。此外，已
完成購買第二塊大面積土地，以支持  Gigafab 叢集擴張，服務智慧型手機、
AI 及  HPC 客戶。  
日本 方面，熊本 Fab 23 於 04/2024 投產， 12/2024 起出貨，月產能 40K 。第
二座晶圓廠 2025 年動工，採用 N3 技術，預計 2028 年量產 。預計未來兩
廠合計月產能上看 100K 。  
德國 方面 ，ESMC 於 4Q24 動 工，預 計2027 年底量產 ， 月產能40K，採 N28/22
及 N16/12 製程。  
中國 方面，南京廠持續擴充 N16/12 產能，並已取得美國商務部一年授權以
維持現狀營運。  
海外晶圓廠起始成本高，預期影響 2026 年毛利率 2~3 個百分點：  
海外晶圓廠的起始成本高於台積電在台灣的晶圓廠，因海外晶圓廠規模較
小，整體供應鏈的成本較高，且與台灣成熟的半導體生態系相比，海外半導
體生態系仍處於早期階段。  
台積電在美國亞利桑那州和日本熊本都將分階段建設多座晶圓廠，因此當
第一階段晶圓廠的獲利能力開始提高時，第二階段晶圓廠就會開始建設，隨
著日本熊本廠區與美國亞利桑那廠區的進一步擴張，海外廠區對毛利率影
響將更加明顯，預計 2026 年海外廠對毛利率稀釋影響將達 2-3 個百分點。  
長期而言，隨著公司在美國亞利桑那州投資計劃開始發酵，台積電預計未來
五年海外廠區產能擴張將稀釋公司毛利率，初期預計每年將稀釋 2-3 個百分
點，而後期將擴大至 3-4 個百分點。公司目標是透過管理達最小化成本差
距，彈性定價策略，和客戶分擔成本，及利用地域上的靈活性，繼續與各國
政府密切溝通合作，以降低成本。  
因此，即便在台灣以外增加產能，相信長期毛利率達 53% 以上仍是可實現，
具持續性且健全的股東權益報酬率 (ROE) 能優於 25%，同時為股東帶來盈利
增長。目前來看，各廠都符合原先規劃的進度。台積電勢必會增加海外廠的
投資，雖然成本高於台灣，除地緣政治的因素外，台灣本身的土地和水電等
基礎條件也受到限制。  
台積電處於一個資本密集型的行業，因此公司需要有非常高的毛利率才能
獲得可持續、健康的回報，這就是公司制定定價策略的原因。公司已經在與
主要客戶討論此事，目前進展順利。  
台積電 N2 製程採用 GAA 架構， 2025 年底量產， 2026 年營收貢獻明顯增
加：

7 群益投顧 
台積電 N2 已在 2Q26 貢獻營收，預計 2H26 營收將會更明顯成長。台積電
預計其 N2 與 A16 先進製程家族的產能，在 2026 至 2028 年間的年複合成長
率（ CAGR ）將高達約 70%。N2 製程受惠於 HPC 與 AI 晶片強烈需求，首年
晶圓產出比 N3 首年高出 45% 。  
N2 採 GAAFET 奈米片架構並導入背面電軌設計， N2 已經於 4Q25 開始量
產； N2P 則計畫如期於 2H26 開始量產，開始貢獻較明顯營收。相較 N3E ，
在相同功率下，速度提升 10~15% ；在相同速度下，功耗降低 25~30% 。 N2P
與 N2X 規劃於 2026 年推出。超高效能三層金屬 -絕緣體 -金屬（ UHP3MIM ）
具超過 500fF/ μm2 的電容密度，進一步提升 PPA 的 N2X 和 N2U 分別計畫
於  2027 年及 2028 年量產。 N2U 是 N2P 的延伸技術，採用設計與技術協同
優化，為 AI/HPC 及智慧型手機應用提供一個均衡的選擇。相較於 N2P，N2U 
增加 3~4% 的速度、 8~10% 功耗降低，邏輯密度提升達 3%。  
台積電 A16™ 是採奈米片 (Nanosheet) 電晶體技術，並有 SPR 超級電軌技術，
相對 N2P ， A16 在相同電壓 Vdd 下，速度增快 8~10% ，或在相同速度下，
功耗降低 15~20% ， 晶片密度(Chip Density) 提升高達 1.07~1.10 倍，預 計2H26
量產。  
A14 製程目標於 2028 年量產，為第二代埃米級製程，也是第二代奈米片
（ NanoSheet ）電晶體，是從 N2 跨越 4 個節點的躍升，在性能和功耗方面
帶來顯著優勢 。 與N2 製程相比 ，A14 在相同電壓 Vdd 下 ， 速度增快10~15% ，
或在相同速度下，功耗降低 25~20% ，晶片密度 (Chip Density) 提升高達
1.2~1.23 倍。 A14 技術開發正步入正軌，進展順利。內部產品試產工具展示
接近  90% 的元件性能，以及接近  90% 的  256Mb SRAM 良率。 A14 生產
基地將落在台中 Fab25 及新竹 Fab20 ，於  2027 年開始試產，目標 3Q28 進
入量產，初期月產能規劃約 50~55K/ 月萬片，晶背供電版本則將在 2029 年
量產。  
A13 和  A12 ，作為  A14 家族的延伸。透過創新的  97% 光學縮小，實現超
過  6% 的晶片面積節省。透過持續的設計技術共同優化 （ DTCO ），A13 還
能推動更好的性能與功耗效率提升。 A13 的設計規則是 A14 向下相容，以
確保平滑的  IP 移植 。 也推出 A12， 這將引進超級電軌（ Super Power Rail ）。
A13 和  A12 均計畫於  2029 年開始量產。我們相信  A14 家族對台積電
而言，將是一個比  N2 規模更大、更長壽的節點。預計於 2029 年， A14 後
一年進入生產，並規劃於 2030 年前後推進至 A10 。

8 群益投顧 
N2 快速爬坡，預估台積電 3Q26 營收 1.46 兆元， QoQ+15.17% ，稅後淨利
7,134.05 億元， QoQ+0.97% ，稅後 EPS 27.51 元：  
消費者和對價格敏感的終端市場領域，受到零組件價格上漲以及宏觀經濟
不確定性的影響，正承受著挑戰。因此台積電在業務規劃上保持審慎，儘管
如此，與  AI 相關的需求依然極其強勁。受到先進製程技術的強勁需求支
持，其中 N2 技術的快速產能爬坡。  
台積電  2Q26 的  Days of inventory (DOI ，存貨週轉天數 ) 較  1Q26 增加
7 天至  87 天，主要反映  N2 技術的產能爬坡  。此庫存天數的上升是為滿
足未來  AI 與  HPC 客戶強勁需求所做的積極準備，而非需求放緩  。  
整個 AI 需求強勁，使台積電３ Q25 AI 相關需求持續成長，包括 CPU、 GPU
和 AI 加速器等執行訓練 (training) 和推論 (inference) 功能的 AI 處理器的相關
晶片，不包括網路邊緣或裝置內。其他非 AI 終端應用則溫和復甦。  
2Q26 毛利率方面，台積電預估  3Q26 毛利率將介於  65% - 67% 之間，以
中間值  66% 計算，較  2Q26 的實際毛利率  67.72% 下降約  1.7 個百分
點。這主要是因為 N2 技術的快速爬坡，預期將稀釋毛利率約  3 - 4 個百分
點，此稀釋將被領先技術的強勁需求與持續的成本改善（含生產力提升）部
分抵消。匯率假設方面， 3Q26 預估以美元兌新台幣  32.00 元為假設和
2Q26 實際匯率為  31.60 元相近，預估台積電 3Q26 營收 1.46 兆元，
QoQ+15.17% ，稅後淨利 7,134.05 億元， QoQ+0.97% ，稅後 EPS 27.51 元。  
預估台積電 2026 年營收 5.44
兆元， YoY+42.80% ，稅後獲利 2.76 兆元，
YoY+60.70% ，稅後 EPS 106.46 元：  
台積電管理層認為將有機會再上修  2024~2029 年  AI 加速器營收之年複
合成長率  (CAGR) 至  55% - 59% 。公司整體 2024 年起五年期長期營收成
長率至趨近 25% ( 美元計 )。台積電將 AI 產品定義為 AI GPU 、 AI ASIC 和用
於 AI 訓練和推理的資料中心 HBM 控制等。 2023 年 AI 佔台積電營收 6%，
約 42 億美元， 2024 年和 2025 年 AI 加速器業務佔台積電總營收分別為
14~16% 和 17~19% ， 2025 年約 220 億美元，較 2024 年約 135 億美元，
YoY+63% 。預計 2026 年 AI 加速器相關產品將佔公司營收比重有機會達到
達 25% 以上，比公司之前預期快。 AI 加速器將成為其高效能運算平台 (HPC)
增長最強勁的驅動力，並且是未來幾年台積電整體營收增量增長的最大貢
獻者。  
台積電 2026 年毛利率有利因素： 1)隨著 N3 量產，稀釋效應預計將逐漸減
少，毛利率將有機會高於公司平均。 2)預計 2026 年整體產能利用率將提高。
不利因素： 1)海外晶圓廠由於初期規模較小，放量和成本較高，海外晶圓廠
的獲利能力預計將低於台灣的晶圓廠。隨著時間推移，海外晶圓廠的獲利能
力將逐漸提高。台積電預計海外晶圓廠將稀釋毛利率約 2~3% ，隨著海外晶
圓廠的擴產，後期影響將擴大至 3-4%。2)電費方面，台積電在 2022 年、2023
年和 2024 年分別面臨 15%、17% 和 25% 的電價漲幅。換句話說，過去幾年
電價幾乎翻倍。台積電預計 2026 年上漲電價和其他通貨膨脹成本將影響台
積電至少 1%的毛利率，中東局勢也可能導致特定化學品與氣體價格上漲。
3)N2 開始進入量產，預計將影響全年毛利率約 2~3% ，2H26 N2 的快速產能
爬坡，稀釋毛利率約  3~ 4 個百分點。不過 N2 製程的獲利結構優於 N3。4)

9 群益投顧 
匯率方面，台積電營收 100% 是美元，成本則是 75% 以美元計價，台幣對美
元變化 1%，台積電美元營收將同向影響 1%，毛利率影響 0.4 個百分點，匯
率無法控制，公司盡量降低影響。整體而言，雖然 2H26 毛利率面臨 N2 量
產及海外擴產帶來的結構性成本壓力，理論上將呈現小幅下滑，但考量台積
電具備領先製程定價能力與嚴謹成本控管，預期可有效對中負面影響，使全
年毛利率維持高檔區間，約 65%~67% ，呈現區間震盪，略偏穩定的走勢。  
AI 市場需求持續強勁，儘管  AI 加速器是增量成長的最大貢獻者，但四大
平台（智慧型手機、 HPC、IoT、車用電子）皆會推升營收。即使消費性電子
（如  PC 、 智慧型手機）等價格敏感市場受到零件漲價與總經不確定性影響 ，
但 AI 需求極度強勁。由於 AI 正從「生成式（詢問模式）」轉向「代理式 /
專注型（指令與行動模式）」，大幅增加 Token 的消耗量與運算需求，  
代理式 AI 的出現，帶動  CPU 在  AI 資料中心的需求上揚，除  AI 加速器
之外，也驅動更多的晶圓需求。  這有利台積電，因為無論採取何種  CPU 方
案（不論是  X86 、ARM 架構還是  RISC-V 架構） ，幾乎全都是台積電的客
戶。因此台積電將 2026 年美元營收成長率預期上修， YoY>+40% 。公司預
期 2026 年整體稼動率維持高檔； 2026 年台積電成長動能來自 AI 相關應用，
N3/N2 先進製程放量，使 ASP 提升，先進封裝出貨量增加，預估台積電 2026
年營收 5.44 兆元， YoY+42.80% ，稅後獲利 2.76 兆元， YoY+60.70% ，稅後
EPS 106.46 元。 2026 年全年稅率將為 17~18% 。

13 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

友訊 (2332 TT) 宣布成立友訊機器人公司，預期與上緯合作的四足機器人可望 2026 年
底 出 貨 ， 自 行 研 發 的 陪 伴 機 器 人 將 於 2Q27 登場 ， 機器人一定要連網、管理，連線用
WiFi 、 4G 或是 WiFi Halow ， 網 路 連 線 就 是 友 訊 的 專 長 ， 目 前 四 足 機 器 人 已 有 日 本 社
區 開 發 商 客 戶 ，後 續 將 結 合資 訊 安 全 以 及 大 語 言 模 型 ，推 出 陪 伴 機 器 人 ，目標搶攻 10
萬 台 起 跳 的 市 場； 展 望 未 來 ，網 通 本 業 已 見 起 色 ， 並 且 已 跨 進 具 有 成 長 想 像 空 間 的 機
器 人 產 業 ， 且 目 前 在 日 本 市 場 已 有 斬 獲 ， 未 來 將 持 續 觀 察 機 器 人 相 關 業 務 進 展 ， 但 目
前 評 價 並 未 偏 低 ， 維 持 Neutral 建議 。

友 訊 搶 進 AI 機 器 人 ：  
友訊 (2332 TT) 宣 布 成 立 友 訊 機 器 人 公 司 ， 預 期 與 上 緯 合 作 的 四 足 機 器 人 可
望 2026 年 底 出 貨 ， 自 行 研 發 的 陪 伴 機 器 人 將 於2Q27 登 場 ； 友 訊 從 2025 年
開 始 研 究 機 器 人 使 用 場 域 ， 由 於 機 器 人 一 定 要 連 網 、 管 理 ， 連 線 用WiFi 、
4G 或是 WiFi Halow ，網 路 連 線 就 是 友 訊 的 專 長 ，連 線 模 組 由 友 勁(6142 TT)
台南廠負責 ODM 製 造 ， 3Q26 起 推 出 四 足 機 器 人 、 小 型 四 足 機 器 人 、 機 器
狗、人 形 機 器 人 與 巡 檢 機 器 人，目 前 四 足 機 器 人 已 有 日 本 社 區 開 發 商 客 戶 ，
因為日本現在熊害嚴重，需要機器狗驅趕熊，除了一次性出售機器人收入
之 外 ， 友 訊 還 提 供 軟 體 與 服 務 ， 服 務 是 按 年 收 費 訂 閱 制 。 
除了四足機器人之外，友訊計畫推出陪伴機器人，結合資訊安全以及大語
言 模 型 ， 第 一 代 鎖 定 30 歲 上 下 的 單 身 男 女 ， 提 供 諮 詢 的 功 能 ， 第 二 代 陪 伴
機器人希望鎖定年長者，由於機器人身上有多個 Sensor 可 以 測 量 生 命 徵
象，能檢測使用者是否跌倒或遭遇意外，能夠做到更好的家戶照護，陪伴
機 器 人 目 標 搶 攻 10 萬 台 起 跳 的 市 場 。 
展望 2026 年 營 運 ， 由 於 具 有 非 紅 供 應 鏈 的 優 勢 ， 專 攻 政 府 相 關 標 案 ， 在 日
本 、 新 加 坡 、 泰 國 已 出 貨 多 個 案 子 ，2H26 營 運 將 優 於 1H26 ， 預 估 2026 年
營收 163.38 億 元 ， YoY+20.99% ， 稅 後 淨 利 1.62 億元， EPS0. 33 元。  
網通本業已見起色，並且已跨進具有成長想像空間的機器人產業，且目前
在日本市場已有斬獲，未來將持續觀察機器人相關業務進展，但目前評價
並 未 偏 低 ， 維 持 Neutral 建議 。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投資評等 係 「 首 次 」 給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時 ， 其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較 3 個
月目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為三個等級，定義如
上 。 爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」 及 「 買 進 」 均 有 upside 目 標 價 。 兩 者 差 別 僅 在 於 ， 首 次 評 等
時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「買進」及「強力買進」之投資評等，就是責任的
開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及 股 價 變 化 ，
直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ，
研 判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。
我們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來
預測之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投
資 目 的 或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的 ，
既非對投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提
供詢價服務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值
及收益，可能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投
資 來 得 少 的 情 形 。

### Page 1

During the 2026 WAIC (world artificial intelligence conference, July 17 - 20, 
Shanghai), Mitac showcased comprehensive AI infrastructure across CPU server, GPU 
server, storage, liquid-cooled rack, air-cooled rack, etc. reflecting its strong R&D to 
provide customized AI configuration for enterprises or tier-2 CSPs (link). As we 
highlighted in June, we raised global AI infrastructure spending (Global Server TAM): 
(1) AI servers implied AI chips were raised by 14% / 22% / 14% in 2026E-28E, (2) US 
CSPs’ AI capex was raised by 8% / 27% / 25% in 2026-28E, and (3) China CSP’s AI 
capex was raised by 37% / 44% / 55% in 2026E-28E, reflecting our positive view on 
the ongoing AI infrastructure upcycle. Key products Mitac showcased in 2026 WAIC:

### Page 2

(1) AI liquid-cooled rack, G4826Z5, powered by AMD MI355X GPU, aiming for massive 
AI model training with sustained high-throughput and minimal latency, (2) AI air-cooled 
rack, G8825Z5, powered by 8 AMD MI350X GPU per server, and one rack could contain 
four servers, aiming for training and inferencing, (3) AI liquid-cooled rack under OCP 
ORv3 standard, including 14 HPC servers (C2811Z5), powered by AMD EPYC 9005 series 
processors, offering high-performance servers with optimizing energy utilization and 
flexible rack configuration to secure a faster roll out, (4) high-density storage rack, 
TS70A-B8056, through the collaboration across hardware and software, the storage 
rack could facilitate computing efficiency and agentic AI workload operation.

Earnings revision: We reduce 2026 / 27E net income by 12% / 5% and keep our 
2028-30E estimates largely unchanged. The net income cut is mainly on lower revenues 
and GM, reflecting the gradual ramp up of new capacity in 2026, along with GM being 
affected by (1) AI server racks carrying lower GM compared to the existing networking 
racks, (2) new capacity ramping up carrying higher depreciation and lower yield rate, 
and (3) high raw material costs. The company’s GM declined from 12.6% in 2023 to 9.2% 
in 1Q26, and we model a similar GM level in coming years. Upside catalysts to our GM 
estimates include: (1) faster-than-expected capacity and yield rate ramp up, (2) 
higher-than-expected customization level of AI servers, (3) higher-than-expected 
customers exposure to enterprises, and (4) faster-than-expected raw material costs 
decline.

Valuation: We continue to derive our target price via near-term PE and roll over from 
2026E to 2027E. We continue to derive our target P/E multiple from peers’ trading P/E 
vs. forward year EPS growth (methodology unchanged with updated numbers), which is 
at 13.3x (vs. 14.7x of our previous TP implied 2027E PE), reflecting the recent sector 
de-rating under AI server model transition. Our new target PE multiple continues to be 
above Mitac’s historical avg. PE of 12.1x, reflecting our positive view on its AI products 
expansion. TP at NT$117 (13.3x 2027E PE vs. 19% NI YoY in 2027-28E on avg.), vs. 
NT$137 previously. Maintain Buy.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

2Q26: Earnings upside from margins, not just revenue - Yageo's 2Q revenues of 
NT$44.5bn (+16% QoQ/+36% YoY) has already surpassed mgmt.'s "moderate 
growth" guidance issued on 1Q26 earnings call, driven by broad-based demand 
across both standard and specialty products. We believe the additional earnings 
upside in 2Q26 will come from margins rather than the revenue line alone. We 
previously estimated 2Q26E GPM at 38.5% (+0.4ppt QoQ) and OPM at 25.5% 
(+0.3ppt QoQ), but expect further upside to reflect the lagged flow-through of 
pricing adjustments and better operating leverage at higher UTR levels. We also 
believe the effects of pricing adjustments will gradually be reflected in margins in 
future quarters.
3Q26E: Price hike begins flowing through - Per local media, Yageo has initiated a 
broad-based capacitor price hike effective Jul 1, covering MLCC, tantalum, 
aluminum capacitors, representing approximately 50% of total revenues. Our 
channel checks also indicate ASP increases of 15-25% compared to Jun levels. We 
expect the adjustments to begin materializing in 3Q26 ASPs, supporting potential 
GPM expansion beyond our current estimates. Key topics we expect mgmt. to 
address during the earnings call include: 3Q26 revenue/margin guidance, BB ratio 
and UTR trajectory, capacity expansion schedule and thresholds, AI revenue mix 
evolution, channel inventory health, future pricing strategy and sustainability of 
non-AI & consumer demand.
Fundamental thesis intact - Yageo's shares have corrected c.39% in Jul, 
underperforming TAIEX's down c.5% over the same period. We believe the recent 
selloff reflects a series of non-fundamental factors: crowded position unwinds 
across Asia passive component names, forced deleveraging from Korean ETF 
liquidations, rather than any deterioration in Yageo's fundamental earnings 
Buy

Yageo (2327.TW)

Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,500, based on 40x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 40x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.

Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

Henry Zhao
Research Associate
Henry.Zhao@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7731
Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
Google’s higher AI/cloud capex through 2027 should ease
concerns over a near-term slowdown in AI infrastructure
spending. The TPU and Axion ramp also reinforces a positive
outlook for the broader semiconductor and AI server supply
chain.
Key takeaways from today's Alphabet (GOOGL.O, covered by Brian Nowak) 2Q26

earnings call:
• Capex raised again as AI capacity remains constrained: Alphabet lifted
FY26 capex guidance to US$195-205bn from US$180-190bn, driven by faster
compute deployment and stronger demand from Google Cloud customers
and internal businesses. 2Q26 capex reached US$44.9bn, up 26% QoQ and
100% YoY. Technical infrastructure spending split: 60% servers, 40% data
centers and networking. Management reiterated a significant FY27 capex
increase, implying continued FCF pressure.

• TPU monetization begins, with 2027 the key ramp year: 2Q26 marked the
first quarter of TPU system deliveries to customer data centers and related
revenue recognition. Only a small portion of contracted sales is expected in
2026, with the vast majority in 2027. External supply will depend on
customer demand, industry constraints, and Alphabet’s internal AI
requirements.
• Alphabet is broadening and optimizing its full compute stack — including
TPUs, NVIDIA accelerators, Axion CPUs, servers, networking, data centers,

and third-party capacity — to lower workload costs and improve capital
efficiency, with its new agent-optimized Axion CPU delivering approximately
30% better performance per dollar than peer products.
Our thoughts: We think Alphabet's commentary is positive for our cloud semi
names, including Aspeed (5274.TW) and Montage (6809.HK), especially with their
continuous AI/cloud investment into 2027, and should help alleviate investors'
concerns of slower AI investment. Besides, Alphabet's positive commentary around

TPU and Axion CPU should bode well for the AI supply chain, including TSMC
(2330.TW), ASE (3711.TW), MediaTek (2454.TW), KYEC (2449.TW), Aspeed, MPI
(6223.TWQ), Winway (6515.TW), and Hon Precision (7769.TW). See more in our
recent cloud semis update.
Alphabet results note: Alphabet Inc.: 4 Learnings and 2 Catalysts (23 Jul 2026)

companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
Intel's  commentary suggests a positive outlook for cloud semi
names in Greater China, driven by strong customer demand for
x86 server CPU.  We are OW on Aspeed and Montage.
Key takeaways from Intel's 2Q26 earnings call:
• Data Center and AI (DCAI) continues to see demand acceleration across
cloud and enterprise, as customers increasingly recognize the critical role of
x86 CPUs in AI infra. The outlook was strengthened by additional strategic

customer wins and long-term agreements in 2Q. The challenge now is to
grow supply to meet customer demand. Intel's custom ASIC (Application-
Specific Integrated Circuit) business currently has a US$2bn run rate and
Intel management said it expects a US$4bn run rate in the near future.
• Customers continue to signal a strong and sustainable spending
environment, driven by AI compute demand. Industry-wide supply
constraints across wafers, memory, and substrate remain the dominant

challenge. The outlook for server CPU demand has improved, and Intel
management now forecasts strong double-digit growth for the server CPU
industry globally this year and next, with momentum extending into 2028.
Our thoughts: We believe Intel's commentary is positive for the Greater China cloud
semi names that we cover, including Aspeed and Montage,  especially given Intel's
positive feedback on strong customer demand. For more details, see  our recent

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
July 27, 2026 06:36 AM GMT

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Aspeed Technology (5274.TWO)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions:
n Cost of equity of 9.8% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.3 beta)
n Medium-term growth rate of 21.4%
n Terminal growth rate of 5.5%
n Cash payout ratio of 85%
Risks to Upside
n Stronger cloud demand
n Faster-than-expected spec migration
n Mild competition
Risks to Downside
n Softening cloud demand
n Slower-than-expected spec migration

n Intensified competition
n Further policy tightening in China
Montage Technology Co Ltd (6809.HK)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions:
n 8.4% CoE (1.2 beta, 3.0% risk-free rate, and 4.5% risk premium)
n 30% payout ratio
n 19.3% medium-term growth rate
n 4% terminal growth rate
These assumptions reflect cloud capex growth, DRAM interface technology migration, and
Montage's strong position in China's datacenter semi localization. We then apply an exchange

rate of 1.15 HKD:1 RMB, assuming no H-share discount vs the A-share.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected phase-out of US peers
n Faster-than-expected spec migration
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected cloud demand
n Slower-than-expected DRAM interface technology migration
n Delay in new product launches

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

### Page 1

Airtac (1590.TW)
2Q26 Beat with Solid OPM; Guidance Raised Again; Buy
CITI'S TAKE 
Airtac delivered another broad-based beat in 2Q26, with net income 
9%/14% above our/BBG est. The key positive was solid OPM of 35.1%, 
supported by stronger UT, a better mix and scale benefit, despite an 
estimated 1.8ppt drag from higher social security expenses. Mgmt raised its 
FY26 sales/OPM guidance again. While normal seasonality could lead to a 
q/q decrease in sales in 3Q, we believe continued share gains and new 
product launches should support a solid 2H26. We raise our earnings est 7-
8% for 26-28E yet keep our PT unchanged at NT$1750 based on 27.5x NTM 
P/E. Stay Buy.
2Q26 earnings beat our/BBG est by 9%/14%; record OPM despite higher social 
security costs— Airtac reported 2Q25 net income of NT$3.3bn, up 63% y/y and 24% 
q/q, topping our/BBG est. 
Sales rose 36% y/y thanks mostly to a broad-based strong 
demand across most end-applications. GPM expanded 3.7ppt y/y to 49.7%, 
exceeding our/BBG forecasts, driven by higher UT, efficiency improvement and 
better mix. That along with scale benefit offset the negative impact from higher 
social security costs and drove OPM to reach a record 35.1% since listed.     
Guidance raised again; solid 2H26 outlook supported by share gains and new 
products — Airtac raised its FY26 sales guidance to >20% y/y in RMB terms, up from 
mid to high teens previously, and lifted OPM guidance to 34% from 33%. Mgmt. 
expects the overall China’s pneumatic market to rise mid to high single digit y/y in 
2026E, with Airtac potentially delivering an additional 10ppt of growth through 
continued share gains. Several new products scheduled to launch in 4Q26 including 
semi-focused pneumatics and three new electrical controller series should provide 
additional growth drivers, particularly, in the medium term. 
Broad-based demand recovery across major end applications — Mgmt expects 
DD growth across electronics, battery, auto and traditional industries in 2026. 
Electronics should benefit from smartphone upgrades and AI-related automation 
investment, while battery growth should extend into 2027 on domestic capacity 
expansion and ESS investment. Auto growth is supported by share gains while 
traditional industries benefit from equipment replacement subsidies. Separately, 
linear guide sales rose 20% y/y in 1H26. UT improved from <20% in 2025, to >40% 
now and should reach 50% by end-2026, supporting profitability. (continued)

Raise 2026-2028E EPS by 8%/7%/7%; keep TP unchanged at NT$1750 — We
raise our sales estimates 5-7% for 2026-2028E, reflecting the stronger 2Q26
results, higher company guidance and a more constructive demand outlook. We
also raise our OPM assumptions to factor in stronger utilization, op leverage and
continued efficiency improvement. We keep our TP unchanged at NT$1750, based
on 27.5x 2H26-1H27E EPS (vs. prior 30x 2Q26-1Q27E). We lower our target

multiple to reflect the stock’s YTD rally and a more normalized valuation as the
current upcycle progresses, offsetting the benefit from our earnings upgrades and
the rollover of the valuation period.  We remain constructive on Airtac’s broad-
based demand recovery, structural market share gains and margin expansion
potential. Stay Buy.

### Page 5

Airtac (1590.TW)
28 July 2026 Citi Research
5
Airtac
Company description
Airtac is the second-largest pneumatic component supplier in China,
accounting for a 30% market share as of 2025, according to the company. It
manufactures and sells pneumatic components including cylinders, filter
regulator lubricators, air and mechanical valves, and linear guides under the
AirTAC brand, which are used in automated production applications across a
wide range of industries including electronics, battery, machinery, packaging,

and automotive.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Airtac as: 1) we view it as a major beneficiary of a
secular FA automation trend in China, which should drive a multi-year growth
trajectory; 2) we expect its quality products with competitive pricing to drive
more share gains in the pneumatics market during macr uncertainties; and 3)
we think their expansion into the application for semi industry will further
unlock its growth potential in the next 2-3 years.

Valuation
We derive our target price of NT$1,750 for Airtac by using a P/E methodology,
as we believe the margin and earnings growth outlooks will be the key drivers
of the stock. We apply a P/E of 27.5x for 2H26-1H27E, which we derive from
the mid range of prior upcycle valuation 25-35x during 2021 in view of a
strong upcycle with solid FA demand and ongoing share gains.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the Airtac stock from reaching our

target price include: 1) a softer-than-expected China macro economy,
resulting in downside to our sales forecasts; 2) intensifying competition
leading to gross margin pressures; and 3) slower-than-expected progress of
its linear guide business, causing low utilization of capacity.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

2Q26 miss on major customer’s model transition: UMT 2Q26 revenues were up 
76% YoY to NT$902m, with rising LEO satellite revenues contribution, while the 
revenues is 7% / 12% behind our / Bloomberg consensus given its major customer’s 
model transition. 2Q26 GM was at 55.6% (vs. 2Q25/ 1Q26 at 43.4%/ 58.0%) with 
improving YoY driven by better product mix, while lower than our estimates / 
Bloomberg consensus, given the impact from model transition. We expect to see 
sequential GM improvement ahead on rising LEO satellite contribution and less 
impact from model transition. 2Q26 Opex ratio was at 28.8%, or higher than our 
estimates / Bloomberg consensus at 26.7%/ 27.3%, and we attribute it to smaller

revenues scale. Overall, the OP income remains strong in YoY at +158%, while decline

transition.

Earnings revision: We factor in UMT 2Q26 results, and revise down 2026E GM by 
2.4ppts to reflect the impact on model transition in 2Q - 3Q26. We continue to expect a 
strong growth ahead driven by LEO satellites launches and specification upgrade, and 
thus keep our estimates in 2027 - 28E largely unchanged. The blended GM would 
continue to expand on product mix upgrade toward LEO satellites from traditional RF 
products for telecom operators.

Valuation: We continue to derive our target price based on a discounted P/E, and based 
on 2029E (methodology unchanged). We continue to derive the target P/E multiple from 
the average PEG&M ratio of peers in the satellite supply chain, and we remove outliers 
(telecom operators who face competition from LEO satellite operators). Our target 
2029E discounted P/E multiple is at 37.0x (unchanged) which is based on peers’ avg. 
PEG&M, and UMT’s 2029-30E avg. NI YoY and OPM. We apply the 37.0x multiple to 
2029E EPS and discount it back to 2027E via a COE of 7.8% (no change). Our target price 
is at NT$2,513 (unchanged), implying 60x 2027E P/E, which is above the company’s 
average +1 stv. P/E of 40x, reflecting our positive view on UMT’s product mix upgrade 
toward LEO satellites. Maintain Buy.

### Page 4

Price target risks and methodology - UMT 
Valuation: We use a discounted P/E methodology and apply a 37.0x target P/E multiple 
to UMT’s 2029E EPS, discounting it back to 2027E at a COE of 7.8% (beta 1.2x, risk-free 
rate 1.6% and market risk premium at 5.1%), which leads to our 12-month target price 
of NT$2,513. The target P/E is based on the average PEG&M ratio of peers in the 
satellite supply chain. We are Buy-rated on UMT.  
Key Risks: Slower-than-expected LEO satellite deployment; Potential competition from 
new-entrant suppliers; LEO satellite operators manufacturing in-house components.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 27 Jul 2026 close.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that

### Page 1

Jentech

Buy: heat spreader momentum still solid 
with ASP growth; MCL more likely in 2H28

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 4,000 TWD | Price: 3,370 TWD

Ongoing ASP growth for next-gen GPU heat spreader 
Our latest checks suggest that (1) heat spreaders will still be used in Nvidia’s Rubin Ultra 
generation and (2) ASP for the Rubin/Rubin Ultra generations will be US$100/US$200 
(vs. US$30 for Blackwell Ultra), driven by design upgrades, such as the removable lid 
design for Rubin Ultra, and higher quality requirements, such as flatness. 
Stronger 2Q26 sales; expect ongoing margin expansion 
Jentech’s 2Q26 sales (+37% QoQ, +40% YoY) were 14%/12% above BofAe/consensus. 
We attribute solid sales mainly to heat spreader ASP hike amid generation migration. 
With stronger sales, we expect its ongoing margin expansion trend to remain intact. We 
project 2Q26 GM/OPM at 44.3%/36.0%, vs. 41.8%/32.0% in 1Q26 and 41.5%/32.8% in 
2Q25. Jentech plans to post 2Q26 results on 12 Aug after market close. We project 
Jentech’s sales to grow 10% QoQ in 3Q26 off a high 2Q26 base and 18% QoQ in 4Q26, 
supported by stronger momentum for next-gen products, such as AMD MI450.  
MCL now more likely in 2H28 for Feynman generation 
Our latest checks also suggest that microchannel lid (MCL) will likely be used from 2H28 
for Nvidia’s Feynman generation, vs. our previous expectation for the Rubin Ultra 
generation. We view supply chain readiness and yield rate as the major reasons for the 
delay. Still, we think Jentech is the enabler for MCL and should be the first MCL supplier. 
On the other hand, we expect limited sales contribution from cold plate/CPO in 2026-28.  
New PO NT$4,000; reit. Buy: top packaging-level cooling name 
We lift our 2026E EPS by 7% and keep 2027E EPS roughly unchanged to reflect still 
solid sales/margin outlook for packaging-level cooling business but cut our 2028E EPS 
by 20% for a later MCL adoption schedule. We trim our PO to NT$4,000 from NT$4,655 
with valuation methodology unchanged at 25x 2H27-1H28E EPS. Reiterate Buy as top 
semi packaging-level component maker with 65%/86% sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28E.

Key Metrics

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
Jentech is the globally leading semi-packaging level cooling 
component (heat spreader, lid, stiffener) manufacturer 
from Taiwan for PC and server. It also offers lead frame for 
auto and other component/connector for consumer 
electronic devices. 
Jentech was founded on 28 March 1987 and was publicly 
listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange on 18 Nov 2009. Its 
manufacturing facilities are located in Taiwan and China 
and its headquarter is located in Taoyuan, Taiwan.

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to Jentech given its leading position 
in semi-packaging level cooling component to ride on 
strong AI market demand and still evolving cooling design. 
We also believe Jentech will be the enabler and first/key 
maker for the most-advanced microchannel lid (MCL) liquid 
cooling design, driving its concrete sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-
28.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
Jentech (XVFSF) 
Our PO of NT$4,000 is based on 25x 2H27-1H28E EPS. We use 25x P/E multiple (around 
1SD above P/E average since IPO) in view of strong AI server market demand, leading 
position in semi-packaging level cooling components, expanding margins, and double-
digit sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28E.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) stronger AI server demand, (2) supply chain's faster 
cooling design migration, (3) faster capacity expansion, (4) favorable FX movement 
(mainly USD/NTD).

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) softer AI server demand, (2) GM pressure due to higher 
raw material cost, (3) supply chain's delayed cooling design migration, (4) slower 
capacity expansion, (5) unfavorable FX movement (mainly USD/NTD) to weigh on GM 
and/or non-operating items.

### Page 1

Vanguard International Semiconductor Co

Healthy power IC demand supports 8” 
pricing inflection at compelling valuation

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 210.00 TWD | Price: 157.00 TWD

Favorable 8” landscape lifts utilization and pricing outlook 
We update our mature foundry supply/demand model, with the discussion on the mature 
12” utilization and pricing outlook in our UMC note. For 8”, we see the landscape in the 
past quarter has been turning even more favorable led by demand strength from power 
IC backlog on higher server/automotive content across IDM outsourcing and fabless. The 
8” supply is also on track to decline 7%/2% in ‘26/27 due to TSMC and Samsung’s 
equipment retirement along with effective capacity loss from Vanguard and Powerchip 
on business upgrade, lifting industry utilization from 81% in ’25 to 87%/91% in ‘26/27. 
PMIC demand strength solidifies GMs even with 12” ramp 
With Vanguard’s 75% sales from power ICs, we estimate its 8” utilization trended better 
than the industry to ~85% in 2Q26 with sales +14% QoQ. We expect the momentum to 
sustain in 3Q26 for another +10% QoQ in sales backed by continued shipment growth, 
modest price hike and favorable FX. On more benign 8” landscape, its utilization could 
reach ~90%/95%+ by 4Q26/4Q27 in our base case, allowing 3-5% QoQ for inflationary 
cost pass through in 2H26 and further strength in ’27 as the bargaining power shifts to 
foundries on tighter 8” BCD process supply. Factoring in 5%/11% YoY 8” price hike in 
4Q26/4Q27 and 13% incremental sales from mature 12” nodes in ‘27, we expect 
Vanguard’s sales to grow 47% YoY in ’27, sufficient to offset a 51% depreciation hike 
from its Singapore fab ramp and sustaining GMs upward trend to 36%/39% in ’27/28. 
Strong 8” pricing underestimated at attractive valuation 
On structurally better 8” supply/demand more than offsetting the burden from the initial 
12” cost, we lift our ‘27/28 EPS from NT$9.97/NT$10.46 to NT$10.60/NT$14.50. We 
keep our Buy rating and lift PO from NT$180 to NT$210 now based on 20x 2027 P/E 
(vs. its 10-32x range and was 18x P/E) on better 8” pricing/UTR outlook. Our earnings 
estimate is 12-61% higher than street as we believe strong pricing power on 8” is 
underappreciated and has upside if it secures more TSMC’s 2nd hand equipment.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Vanguard International Semiconductor Co (Vanguard) is a 
pure 8-inch foundry headquartered in Hsinchu, Taiwan. The 
company has five fabs, one of which is located in Singapore. 
Collectively, the capacity is about 280k wpm currently, and 
the company focuses on fabricating power semiconductors 
and driver IC for both IDM (integrated device 
manufacturers) and fabless customers.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Vanguard as we expect an 
improvement in the overall 8" foundry supply/demand 
landscape from TSMC's retirement of some legacy capacity 
and limited new industry capacity additions. Higher 
utilization and pricing upcycle in 8", coupled with enhanced 
mix from share gain in Power IC, should support sales and 
gross margin expansion.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
Vanguard International Semiconductor Co (VGILF) 
Our PO NT$210 is based on 20x 2027E P/E. The multiple is slightly below the midpoint 
of Vanguard's historical range of 10-32x. In our view, it is to reflect both 1) depreciation 
associated with Singapore fab, and 2) increasing share in Power IC market and 
pricing/utilization uptrend.

Upside risks are: 
(1) Stronger-than-expected end market demand for power management IC (PMIC) and/or 
display driver IC (DDIC) 
(2) Expedited market share gain due to the order shift by customers from China-based 
foundries.

Downside risks are: 
(1) Higher cost burden if customers demand to move the production base to US and/or 
Europe 
(2) Slowdown of growth in auto market where the company has 10-20% revenue 
exposure.

Idea
AirTAC International  | Asia Pacific
2Q26 Beat; 2026 Guidance
Raised Further
The industry up-cycle and AirTAC's own share gain story should
continue to play out. Meanwhile, the stock might outperform in
the near term amid the volatility in AI sentiment. Stay  OW.
2Q26 beat on margins: OPM expanded 1.9ppt, to 35.1% (our estimate: 34.1%;
consensus: 34.5%), via bigger scale and greater efficiency. Thus,  operating and net
profits exceeded our estimates (by 10% and 8%) and consensus (by 9% and 6%). See

Results Summary               .
During the 2Q26 earnings call, management raised its 2026 guidance again (as it
did during the 1Q26 earnings call): It looks for revenue to grow >20% YoY in Rmb
terms (vs. prior mid- to high teens) and for operating margin of 34% (vs. prior 33%).
It sees the pneumatic component up-cycle  being sustained for 2-3 years and that
AirTAC should outgrow the market via share gains (target at 35% share in 2030 vs.
30% in 2025). By segment, it expects  electronics (new specs in smartphones and

overall tech capacity expansion), batteries (Chinese government policies and
overseas expansion), automobiles (its own share gains) and traditional markets
(machine tools, general machinery, packaging, textiles, etc.) to show double digit (%)
growth this year. Energy and lighting should be down (to a narrow extent vs. 2025).
More products to drive stronger growth: To leverage its existing sales team and
customer relationship, AirTAC will continue to introduce more products to the

market in pneumatic components. It is turning more aggressive in  semiconductors
and expects to see more meaningful revenue contribution from 2H27 (will sell some
shared SKUs with other segments first from 2H26), riding the localization trend in
China's semi supply chain (target at Rmb1bn in 10 years vs. annualized ~Rmb150mn
now). Linear guides should continue to gain more traction with improving
profitability (~50% GM by this year-end from ~30% now) amid improving end

demand in coming years (target Rmb3bn in 10 years vs. ~Rmb550mn in 2025).
Electrical controllers will have broader offerings in the high-end segment from late
2026 (target Rmb3bn in 10 years vs. Rmb400mn in 2025). Last, electrical actuators
are likely to be unveiled in 2028/2029.
Stay OW; unchanged price target of NT$2,000 (29x 2027e P/E): The industry up-
cycle and company-specific share gains should bolster earnings growth in 2026-27.
With undemanding valuation (20x 2027e P/E; mid-cycle average 25x) and potentially

Results Summary
2Q26 beat on margins
Off revenue of NT$12,164mn (+21% QoQ/+36% YoY), GM expanded 1.7ppts QoQ, to 49.7%
(vs. our estimate at 48.0% and consensus at 48.7%), mainly driven by higher utilization
and greater production efficiency.
Owing to incremental social security costs in China, opex was higher at NT$1,783mn (opex
ratio 14.7%) vs. our estimate at NT$1,580mn (opex ratio 13.9%) and consensus at NT
$1,607mn (opex ratio 14.2%). Operating profit thus came in at NT$4,265mn (OPM 35.1%),

surpassing our estimate of NT$3,867mn (OPM 34.1%) and consensus at NT$3,912mn
(OPM 34.5%).
With non-op loss of NT$27mn (including PPE disposal loss of NT$127mn, FX gains of NT
$5mn and government subsidy of NT$69mn), net profit of NT$3,311mn (EPS NT$16.54)
was 8% above our estimate of NT$3,059mn (EPS of NT$15.28) and consensus of NT
$3,134mn (EPS of NT$15.61).

6
AirTAC: Valuation Methodology
Our  price target remains unchanged at NT$2,000: This is our base case value,  derived
by applying a target P/E multiple of 29x   to our 2027 EPS estimate (30x previously). We
lowered our targeted multiple to factor in the recent market volatility. We view our
multiple as reasonable as we enter an up-cycle alongside AirTAC's continued share gain
story, slightly below its peak valuation of 30x P/E. Since 2011, the forward P/E multiple

has ranged from 12x to 40x, which we view as consistent with its business cycles. We
believe operating margin is a good cycle indicator,  as it is highly dependent upon operating
leverage.
Bull case scenario value – NT$2,200: We apply a 32x P/E multiple  to our 2027 EPS
estimate (vs. 33x previously). This scenario mainly reflects stronger-than-expected
pneumatic component demand, driving more earnings upside.
Bear case scenario value – NT$950: We apply a 14x P/E multiple  to our 2027 EPS

estimate (unchanged). This scenario mainly factors in a weaker-than-expected macro
environment, affecting capital expenditure across  sectors and reducing overall demand for
pneumatic components.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

ab 28 July 2026
Global Research
First Read
Powertech Technology
FOPLP expansion facing challenges
Q226 results mixed, Q3 sales guided in-line
Q226 EPS of NT$3.00 came in below UBSe/Street on higher tax and minority interests,
despite stronger OP . Q226 sales of NT$23.1 bn was up 8.5% QoQ,  slightly ahead of
UBSe/Street.  GM expanded by 2.4ppt QoQ to 21.8% driven by  ASP hikes, higher
loading, and forex tailwind. Management highlighted Q226's GM  reached a record high

post 2021 and believe its 2026 EPS should be better than prior cycle peak in 2022. For
H226F, it guided Q326F sales to grow single digit % QoQ on sustained memory cycle
and improving ASP , while automotive and industrial  logic demand  remains robust. Q326
GM and EPS are also guided to improve QoQ, and it expects  sales momentum to sustain
into  Q426F.  We forecast Q326 sales to grow 5% QoQ, while GM may  expand 0.3ppt
QoQ to 22.1%. 2026F capex remains unchanged at NT$50bn and we believe Powertech

will use its internal cash flow and bank facilities to support its expansions.
FOPLP expansion facing challenges;  AMD on track to be the first customer
Powertech  remained confident that it will be the first company globally to commence
mass production of FOPLP for AI chips, although both timing and scale of the ramp
appears to be pushed out. The company  cited equipment delivery delays, tool
installation issues, NPI requirements, and qualification processes are the primary

bottlenecks. It now aims to install 2K/mth capacity first, followed by an additional 4-5K/
mth capacity before end-2027 (vs prior 3K/mth by end-2026 and 3K/mth in H127).
Nevertheless, management indicated  it is already  capable of achieving 5x reticle-size and
remains on track to reach its 9x reticle-size target by end-2026. It remains committed  to
support its key customer AMD to ramp FOPLP AI chip before mid-2027, and we believe it
will be mostly for  CPU, while orders for GPU/accelerator has not been confirm.

Partnered with Broadcom on FOPLP and  CPO
Management provided limited details regarding its planned  NT$40bn JV with Broadcom
due to NDA restrictions, but noted the capital injection will be phased in over four
quarters. Powertech said the partnership will also help it to enter into optical
communication field. It has been  working with Broadcom on micro-bump based OE
(optical engine) for  pilot production before end-2026, and aims to integrate the OE with

switch (H227 mass production) and AI chips (late-2028 mass production) in the next
phase. As the JV's capacity  will be dedicated for Broadcom products and its Taiwan
facility focuses on AMD-related programs, we see a potential risk that  Powertech's own
FOPLP capacity may face  utilization challenges during the initial ramp phase.
Valuation:　Raise PT to NT$260, retain Neutral
We revise 2026/27/28E EPS by 1%/1%/0% on better OPM, offset by higher tax and

minority interest from Greatek/Teraprobe. We raise our PT to NT$260 (from NT$240)
with 17x 2027E  PE (from 16x) on improving margin profiles and CPO opportunities. We
will monitor its progress on FOPLP/CPO and price improvement sustainability.

Company Description
Powertech is a semiconductor back-end supplier with 75-80% of its sales from DRAM and
NAND packaging and testing and has been growing its logic back-end business.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Powertech: We value Powertech using PE methodology.
Upside/downside risks: 1) A stronger/weaker pricing environment; 2) slower-than expected

tech end demand and memory market recovery; and 3) faster/slower ramp of FOPLP and CPO
businesses.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 7 月 22 日

1 群益投顧 
大立光 (3008 TT)  公告斥資 5.61 億 元 取 得 土 地 1,247.51 坪，建物 2,672.5 坪，距離
06/2026 底花費 6.28 億元購入 1,461 坪 土 地 間 隔 不 到 一 個 月，這 次 購 入 建 物 與 土 地 可 望
縮 短 大 幅 縮 短 建 廠 時 程 ， 由 於 目 前 處 於iPhone 鏡 頭 生 產 旺 季 ， 產 能 利 用 率 已 滿 載 ， 此
次 購 入 新 廠 房 ， 推 測 新 產 品 光 纖 陣 列 已 通 過 客 戶 認 證 ， 目 前 正 在 建 立 試 產 線 ， 購 入 新
的廠房與建物為 1H27 的量產做好準備 ； 展望後勢 ， iPhone 鏡頭出貨進入傳統旺季，
預期 2H26 營 運 呈 現 逐 季 成 長 趨 勢 ， 法 說 會 上 明 示FAU 零 組 件 進 度 ， 預 期 1H27 可望
量 產，顯 示 公 司 對 FAU 零組件業務表示高度信心，有助於提升本益比，維持 Buy 投資
建議 。

研 究 員 ： 劉 宜 和   
yiho.liu@capital.com.tw

大 立 光 一 個 月 內 二 次 出 手 購 地：  
大 立 光 公 告斥資 5.61 億 元 取 得 土 地 1,247.51 坪 ， 建 物 2,672.5 坪 ， 距 離
06/2026 底 花 費 6.28 億元購入 1,461 坪 土 地 間隔 不 到 一 個 月， 這 次 購 入 建 物
與 土 地 可 望 縮 短 大 幅 縮 短 建 廠 時 程 ， 由 於 目 前 處 於iPhone 鏡 頭 生 產 旺 季 ，
產 能 利 用 率 已 滿 載，此 次 購 入 新 廠 房，推 測 新 產 品 光 纖 陣 列(Fiber Array, FA)
已 通 過 客 戶 認 證 ， 目 前 正在 建 立 試 產 線 ，購 入 新 的 廠 房 與 建 物為 1H27 的量
產 做 好 準 備。  
展望 3Q26 ， 雖 然 iPhone18 標 準 版 手 機 鏡 頭 延 後 出 貨 ， 但 是 首 款 iPhone 摺
疊 機 開 賣 ， 以 及 可 變 光 圈 鏡 頭 較 傳 統7P 鏡 頭 漲 價 ， 皆 有 助 於 推 動 鏡 頭 旺 季
效 應 ， 預 估 3Q26 營收 178.89 億元， QoQ+30.92% ； 由 於 可 變 光 圈 鏡 頭 為 新
產 品 ， 又 因 增 加 多 個 檢 測 工 站 使 良 率 更 低 ， 毛 利 率 將 低 於2Q26 ， 預 估 稅 後
淨利 62.67 億 元 ， QoQ+34.19% ， EPS47.91 元 。  
展望 2026 年， Apple 摺 疊 機 發 售 可 望 減 緩 iPhone 銷 量 下 滑 的 幅 度 ， 力 拼 整
體 iPhone 銷 量 持 平 ；iPhone 半 導 體 零 組 件 多 為 自 製 ，iPhone 抵 抗 成 本 上 揚
能力優於 Android 手 機，故 能 依 照 原 定 計 畫 升 級 高 階 機 種 主 鏡 頭 為 可 變 光 圈
鏡頭，可變光圈鏡頭因為需要多段光圈皆能精準對焦，而增加檢測站數將
導 致 良 率 下 降，因 此 鏡 頭 價 格 較 傳 統 7P 鏡 頭 更 高，預 估 2026 年營收 653.47
億元， YoY+6.87% ， 稅 後 淨 利 238.33 億 元 ， YoY+12.02% ， EPS182.21 元。
未來 FAU 業 務 將 成 為 影 響 本 益 比 高 低 的 重 要 因 素。  
iPhone 鏡 頭 出 貨 進 入 傳 統 旺 季 ， 預 期2H26 營 運 呈 現 逐 季 成 長 趨 勢 ， 法 說 會
上明示 FAU 零 組 件 進 度 ， 預 期 1H27 可 望 量 產 ， 顯 示 公 司 對 FAU 零 組 件 業
務 表 示 高 度 信 心 ， 有 助 於 提 升 本 益 比 ， 維 持Buy 投 資 建 議 。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係 「 首 次 」 給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時 ， 其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個
月目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為 四 個等級，定義如
上 。 爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。 差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」 或「區間操作」 之投資評
等，就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基
本 面 及 股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ，
研 判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。
我們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來
預測之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投
資 目 的 或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的 ，
既非對投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提
供詢價服務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值
及收益，可能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投
資 來 得 少 的 情 形 。

欣興法說前財報預估 
預估 2H26 隨 ABF 供需逐步趨緊，漲價幅度將加劇

| 財報訊息   
欣興即將於 7/29(三)舉行法說會， 2Q26營收季增 12.48%至421.2億
元，符合預期，市場普遍預估毛利率介於 22~24%，EPS約4.16元，
後續將關注公司對於整體ABF產業供需狀況展望及預期漲價幅度。 
| 營運現況  
2Q26營收符合預期，預估毛利率顯著提升 ：2Q26營收季增12.48%
至421.2億元，營收達成率約101.8%，主因漲價效益及光復廠新產能
逐步開出，毛利率部分，預估於ABF、BT持續漲價及產品組合優化
下，將提升4.4ppts至22.36%，營益率12.12%，稅後EPS 4.16元。 
| 營運展望  
預估3Q26營收季增7.93%至454.6億元，漲價幅度將加劇：研究部預
估3Q26營收季增7.93%至454.6億元，2H26除漲價效應將更為顯著，
HDI業務亦將受惠同業VR200 Compute Board測試結果不佳，公司預
期可獲取額外份額，加上光模組市場需求強勁，預計2026年底前將
新增30%~45%的既有桃園廠區產能以因應光模組需求，另隨 2H26 
ABF產業重回供不應求局面，預估2026年ABF、BT載板價格亦將逐
季上漲約10-15%，並持續擴充光復廠高階ABF新產能，預估3Q26毛
利率將延續上升趨勢，提升2.69ppts至25.05%，稅後EPS達5.01元。

本次報告：法說會

穩懋 (3105 TT) 
獲利下修

永豐觀點 
雖 2Q26 毛利率與 3Q26 展望低於預期，短期評價下修，但穩懋已同時具備手機、
低軌道衛星與光通訊三大成長題材，隨 Optical 量產放大與 Infra 比重提升，中長
期仍具備一定成長動能。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：考量 2Q26 毛利率表現及 3Q26 毛利率展望低於原先預期，反映短期
產品組合與獲利率改善速度不如先前樂觀，研究部下修本業毛利率預估值， 2026
年 EPS 調整主要受業外獲利影響 ，2026 年 EPS 7.22 元上修至 7.44 元 ， 預估2027
年 EPS 由 11.24 元下調至 8.58 元。惟公司除既有手機 PA 業務外，目前亦具備低
軌道衛星及光通訊雙題材， 中長期成長動能仍在， 尤其Infra 受惠 LEO 與航太需求
持續擴張，Optical 亦自 2Q26 末開始量產並於 2H26 逐步放量，產品組合正持續
改善。因此，雖短期毛利率修正使評價下調，但考量中長期成長方向未變，研究部
仍維持買進投資建議，目標價由 640 元下調至 395 元(2027 年 EPS *46X)。

ESG 評析 
穩懋企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

2

公司簡介：穩懋成立於 1999 年 10 月，目前為全球最大砷化鎵晶圓代工廠，市佔
率 50~60%， 工廠位於林口以及龜山 ， 月產能43,000 片。2Q26 產品組合為 Cellular 
35-40%、Infra 30 -35%、Wi-Fi 10 -15%、Optical 10 -15%、others 5% ，其中
Optical 部分，手機 3D Sensing 應用約 60%，Data center 約 40%。毛利率
Infra>Others> 公司 平 均>Wi-Fi>Cellular 。公 司 主要 客戶 包含Broadcom、
Murata、唯捷創芯以及 Skyworks，競爭對手主要為宏捷科、三安光電。 
2Q26 營運符合預期：2Q26 營收 52.57 億元(+14.5%QoQ，+39.1%YoY)，稼動
率約 65%，各業務別觀察，Cellular 2Q26 季增個位數，Infra 季成長幅度最明顯，
Optical 為四大業務中季增幅度最大，Wi-Fi 則因 router 需求仍弱而下滑。個體毛
利率 32.6%(+0.5ppts QoQ)，合併毛利率 28.2%，營益率 14.1%，業外貢獻 4.47
億元，主要為匯兌利益 1.44 億元及權益法認列 2.77 億元，稅後淨利 9.77 億元
(+83.3%QoQ，虧轉盈 YoY)，稅後 EPS 2.30 元。 
3Q26 營收預估成雙數季增：展望 3Q26，公司預期在美系手機與高階 Android 新
機發表帶動下，Cellular 將延續旺季拉貨動能；Optical 方面，PD 已於 2Q26 末開
始量產，3Q26 出貨量將進一步放大，季增幅度可望為各產品線最佳；Infra 則仍
有多項航太與衛星相關專案持續進行 ， 成長動能僅次於Optical。 相對之下 ，Cellular
與 Wi-Fi 預期僅維持持平至小幅成長。公司展望3Q26 營收將季增 low-teens，毛
利率有望維持 low-thirties， 反映手機旺季與光通訊量產帶動產品組合進一步改善 。
研究部預估 3Q26 營收 58.41 億元(+11.1%QoQ，+30.2%YoY)，毛利率 31.4%，
營益率 17.3%，稅後淨利8.67 億元(-11.3%QoQ，-19.0%YoY)，稅後EPS 2.04元。

### Page 3

2H26 進入傳統旺季， 高階手機銷售穩健：展望 2026 年，穩懋營運將由Cellular、
Infra 與 Optical 三大主軸驅動。Cellular 方面，雖然 2H26 手機旺季仍受記憶體
缺貨與終端價格調漲壓力影響 ， 但公司長期耕耘中高階Android 與美系手機市場 ，
高階機種 PA 與 Wi-Fi PA 拉貨狀況與往年差異不大，因此 Cellular 仍可維持穩健
表現。Infra 方面，航太與 LEO 專案持續推進，且目前衛星營收已占 Infra 比重約
五成，隨 D2C、下一代衛星部署與更高頻寬需求增加，未來衛星比重有望持續提
升；GaN 亦將隨著高功率、長距離傳輸需求增加，成為 Infra 未來的重要成長方
向。Optical 方面，公司表示 2026 年 Driver IC 比重仍較高，但光通訊 PD 專案已
於 2Q26 末開始量產，2H26 將持續 ramp up，預估 2H27CW Laser 發射端產品
將逐步量產，預估 2026 年 Optical 營收比重有機會達 HSD，2027 年可望進一步
達雙位數比重 。 整體而言 ，2026 年公司將受惠手機旺季 、Infra 專案放量與 Optical
量產導入，營收與獲利可望顯著回升。

### Page 4

2Q26 存貨與 DOI 提升 ， 反映提前備料因應2H26 需求：2Q26 存貨上升至 75.84
億元，DOI 達 152 天，管理層表示主要反映原物料備貨增加，而非成品庫存異常
堆積，主因目前全球供應鏈交期仍偏長，加上 GaN、InP 等關鍵基板供應相對吃
緊，因此公司提前備料以確保 2H26 旺季與後續專案出貨穩定。從循環角度觀察，
需留意 2H26、2027 年手機終端銷售是否因記憶體漲價而進一步轉弱，進而使客
戶端拉貨趨於保守。雖然穩懋目前 Cellular 業務仍以中高階 Android 與 Apple 相
關應用為主，受低階手機需求下滑影響相對有限，但若2026~2027 年整體手機市
場持續受到記憶體成本上升壓抑 ，不排除後續手機供應鏈再度進入去庫存循環， 屆
時存貨去化速度與稼動率表現將成為後續觀察重點。 
下修 2026/2027 年獲利預估值：考量 2Q26 毛利率表現及 3Q26 展望低於研究部
先前預期，研究部下修 2026/2027 年毛利率預估值，研究部預估 2026 年營收
214.1 億元(+28.7%YoY)，毛利率 29.4%，營益率 14.4%，稅後淨利 31.55 億元
(+86.3%YoY)，稅後 EPS 7.44 元(原估 7.22 元)，BVPS 103.15 元。預估 2027 年
營收 239.7 億元(+12.0%YoY)， 毛利率32.0%， 營益率17.5%， 稅後淨利36.40 億
元(+14.9%YoY)，稅後 EPS 8.58 元(原估 11.24 元)，BVPS 107.40 元。預估 2028
年營收 268.9 億元(+12.2%YoY)，毛利率 34.2%，營益率 21.1%，稅後淨利 48.7
億元(+33.8%YoY)，稅後 EPS 11.49 元(原估 16.13 元)，BVPS 113.91 元。

欣銓(3264)

Price(07/24):209.5 
T arget Price：280

展望營運後勢 ， 欣銓將持續受惠於 AI 領域產能排擠所帶來的中低
階訂單外溢紅利 ， 進一步鞏固其在通訊與 PC 測試領域的市佔率 ，
且車用與微控制器(MCU)客戶的庫存去化告一段落 ， 亦為整體營運
提供穩健的下檔支撐 。 中長線來看 ， 龍潭新廠於今年下半年的正式
投產 ， 標誌著公司正式跨足高附加價值的 AI ASIC 晶片測試領域 ，
顯示其與晶圓代工大廠及國際策略客戶深度綁定的強烈企圖心 。 此
外 ， 集團首度公開在矽光子(Silicon Photonics)與共同封裝光學元
件(CPO)的完整生態系佈局 ， 透過子公司全智科在湖口新廠的光電
整合測試， 以及瑞豐半導體的先進異質整合封裝技術 ，成功卡位次
世代光通訊供應鏈，隨著高毛利之 AI ASIC 與矽光子測試產能逐
步開出 ， 規模經濟效應與高階機台的滿載稼動率將有助於大幅改善
長期毛利表現，呈現結構性的向上優化。綜合以上，研究部預估欣
銓科技(3264.TT)2027年營業收入為245.21億元 ，Y+30.78%、 毛
利率42.67%，Y+1.92ppt、營業利益率33.33%，Y+2.51ppt、稅
後純益65.83億元，Y+38.91%、EPS為13.88元。考量欣銓營運
結構顯著優化 ， 並成功切入 AI 與光通訊等高階測試站點 ， 獲利成
長具備高度可見性，依據過往本益比區間10至30倍，給予2027
年預估EPS 20倍本益比評價，目標價280元，投資建議為買進。

 近期投資評等 Ardentec欣銓科技(3264.TT)公司簡介 
欣銓科技(Ardentec)成立於1999年，總部位於台灣新竹工業區，為全
球知名的專業半導體測試大廠。公司長期致力於提供高品質的晶圓測試
(Wafer Sort)與成品測試(Final Test)等關鍵服務，測試技術與產品線廣
泛涵蓋微控制器(MCU)、車用電子、射頻積體電路(RFIC)、記憶體及混
合訊號產品等，根據2025年數據，晶圓測試佔公司營收比重達
71.5%，成品測試佔28%。客戶結構方面以Fabless為主，佔比過半達
52%，同時也涵蓋全球各大IDM廠。公司生產據點具備全球化佈局，
除台灣新竹與龍潭外，亦於新加坡、韓國、南京、設有廠區，持續深化
與國際大廠及美系客戶的投產合作。前十大股東持股合計約 27.75%(；
而在國內專業測試領域，主要的台灣測試則包含京元電(2449.TT)與台
星科(3265.TT)等。

融資率 4.94 0.17 營收規模擴大與產品組合優化，第二季獲利優於預期 
欣銓(3264.TT)2Q26受惠於車用及MCU客戶需求回溫，以及同業專注
AI高階測試使得國內通訊客戶訂單產生外溢效應，帶動營運動能顯著增
溫，單季營收達45.34億元，創下歷史新高。觀察各應用領域，通訊產
品在AI周邊需求支撐與轉單效應下持續樂觀，佔比達33.4%；車用
(18.2%)與General MCU(8.5%)亦見到客戶需求回溫與庫存回補的跡
象；PC與消費性電子(9.9%)因同業產能滿載所獲得的轉單紅利仍在延
續，為第二季帶來額外的訂單挹注。在產能利用率方面，第二季稼動率
由前一季的70%初頭提升至73%，有效支撐了本業的獲利能力。隨著
營收規模擴大，加上公司積極處理低稼動率的閒置低階機台，並針對部
分低毛利產品進行報價調漲，帶動毛利率重回40%大關，獲利表現優
於預期。綜合以上，研究部預估欣銓(3264.TT)2Q26營業收入為45.34
億元，Q+13.81%/Y+32.43%、毛利率40.17%，
Q+1.81ppt/Y+3.84ppt、營業利益率30.38%，Q+2.85ppt/Y+3.98ppt、
稅後純益11.56億元，Q+25.05%/Y+105.19%、EPS為2.44元。

第三季龍潭新廠投產，AI ASIC專案帶動雙位數成長 
展望第三季，主要的成長引擎將來自於龍潭新廠的產能開出與先進測試
專案，龍潭廠已於第二季完成客戶驗證與機台裝機，並於7月份正式邁
入量產階段，主要承接晶圓代工大廠的AI ASIC晶圓測試(CP)外溢訂
單，在產能利用率方面，由於AI ASIC屬於高階測試領域，初期即具備
較高的稼動率將有效拉升整體平均稼動率；儘管龍潭廠投產將伴隨高階
設備折舊費用的增加，但因部分廠房與新增人力成本已於量產前提前發
生與認列，對毛利率的稀釋效應十分有限，預期在營收規模持續擴大、
高階機台滿載運作，以及公司陸續處理低階閒置機台的綜效下，第三季
營收將延續第二季的強勁動能，達成季增雙位數的目標，且毛利率亦將
呈現持續攀升的穩健格局。綜合以上，研究部預估欣銓(3264.TT)3Q26
營業收入為49.57億元，Q+9.34%/Y+37.97%、毛利率41.45%，
Q+1.28ppt/Y+5.75ppt、營業利益率31.76%，Q+1.38ppt/Y+7.44ppt、
稅後純益12.65億元，Q+9.48%/Y+52.97%、EPS為2.67元。

AI ASIC與矽光子佈局發酵，資本支出奠定長期基石 
放眼2026年全年，欣銓主要的成長動能包含AI ASIC高階測試、矽光
子(Silicon Photonics)與CPO測試、通訊產品外溢訂單，以及車用與
MCU的溫和復甦。目前龍潭廠共規劃7層樓，第一層無塵室於2H26
投產後，剩餘樓層已發包並將視客戶需求進度於2027年陸續建置，預
期隨著ASIC晶片專案比重逐步放大，未來將為公司持續挹注高附加價
